Canonicalizes same-defect-different-words findings across parallel finders. Plain code (a Set/Map keyed on exact file+line or an exact string) beats this leaf whenever the dedup key is exact — reach for wf-dedup ONLY for fuzzy/semantic merges (same underlying issue described in different words, different citations, or partially-overlapping scope). - **wf-rv**: Cross-reviewer — reads PO/TL/DS artifacts (PRD.md, specs.md, design.md, ui.md, tasks.md), produces review.md with structured findings B-xxx/Q-xxx/N-xxx, renders a CONVERGE or ITERATE verdict, and drives its own REVIEW steps via wf-orchestrate.sh. ([mgallet92i/waterfall](https://github.com/mgallet92i/waterfall/blob/HEAD/agents/wf-rv.md) · ★2)
- **wf-security-reviewer**: Security reviewer for a /run-workflow ticket. Spawned only by the run-workflow orchestrator, with the full brief in the prompt. Never auto-select this agent. ([GionRubitschung/skills](https://github.com/GionRubitschung/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/run-workflow-agents/agents/wf-security-reviewer.md))
- **wf-synth**: Generic SYNTHESIS leaf for Workflow fan-outs. Inherits the session model — synthesis is the ONE stage worth your premium tier. Invoke via agentType:'wf-synth' as the single final stage; never fan this agent out. ([Thrifthunter4you/claude-Autopilot-Kit](https://github.com/Thrifthunter4you/claude-Autopilot-Kit/blob/HEAD/agents/wf-synth.md) · ★5)
- **wf-synthesizer**: Cockpit for the `run_pipeline.synthesize` phase. Dispatched by the run-pipeline controller only — never select this agent for ad-hoc work, and never invoke it from another dispatched phase. Its whole job arrives in the prompt. ([zDud4s/nucleos](https://github.com/zDud4s/nucleos/blob/HEAD/core/assets/nucleos-dev/.claude/agents/wf-synthesizer.md) · ★3)
- **wf-tester**: Generic TEST-RUNNER leaf for Workflow fan-outs. Cheap-tier. Invoke via agentType:'wf-tester' from workflow scripts; not for direct interactive use. Runs a supplied test command verbatim and reports pass/fail — makes NO edits, proposes NO fixes. Serves as an independent re-verify lane for wf-fixer's self-reported test results (a fixer grading its own patch is not independent evidence). ([Thrifthunter4you/claude-Autopilot-Kit](https://github.com/Thrifthunter4you/claude-Autopilot-Kit/blob/HEAD/agents/wf-tester.md) · ★5)
- **wf-tl**: Author of design.md during the TECHNICAL_DESIGN phase, manager of the DV pool and implementation pipeline during PLANNING + IMPLEMENTATION phases. ([mgallet92i/waterfall](https://github.com/mgallet92i/waterfall/blob/HEAD/agents/wf-tl.md) · ★2)
- **wf-tree-orchestrator**: Cockpit for the top-of-branch node of a node tree (routing phase `tree_orchestrate`). Dispatched by the root controller only — never select this agent for ad-hoc work, and never invoke it from a leaf phase. Its whole job arrives in the prompt as a node packet. ([zDud4s/nucleos](https://github.com/zDud4s/nucleos/blob/HEAD/core/assets/nucleos-dev/.claude/agents/wf-tree-orchestrator.md) · ★3)
- **wf-verifier**: Generic ADVERSARIAL VERIFY leaf for Workflow fan-outs. Mid-tier. Invoke via agentType:'wf-verifier' from workflow scripts; not for direct interactive use. Refutes ONE claim per dispatch. ([Thrifthunter4you/claude-Autopilot-Kit](https://github.com/Thrifthunter4you/claude-Autopilot-Kit/blob/HEAD/agents/wf-verifier.md) · ★5)
- **wf-verifier**: Verifier that runs the verification commands for a /run-workflow ticket. Spawned only by the run-workflow orchestrator, with the full brief in the prompt. Never auto-select this agent. ([GionRubitschung/skills](https://github.com/GionRubitschung/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/run-workflow-agents/agents/wf-verifier.md))
- **wf-worker**: Workflow 단계·Agent 도구 위임용 경량 구현 에이전트(Bash·Read·Edit·Write) — 범용 workflow-subagent·general-purpose 의 미사용 도구 정의·스킬 목록(기본 약 47k~48k 토큰) 없이 뜬다. 코드·문서 수정과 명령 실행이 필요한 구현·수정에 Workflow 는 `agentType: 'workflow-policy-plugin:wf-worker'`, Agent 도구는 `subagent_type: 'workflow-policy-plugin:wf-worker'` 로 지정한다(접두를 생략하면 Workflow 는 `agent type ... not found` 오류로 실패함). 스킬이 필요한 작업은 프롬프트에 SKILL.md 경로를 주어 Read 하게 한다. ([starmkk/my-marketplace](https://github.com/starmkk/my-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflow-policy-plugin/agents/wf-worker.md))
- **wfi-auditor-a**: WFI Proposal Quality Auditor for audit cycle 1. Reviews WFI-NNN.md for evidence quality, root cause plausibility, why-why (5 Whys) chain validity, category-appropriate language, concrete proposed changes, and measurable expected effects. Read-only; returns PASS, NEEDS_REVISION, or BLOCKED with classified findings. ([aharada54914/sdd-forge](https://github.com/aharada54914/sdd-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-quality-loop/agents/wfi-auditor-a.md))
- **wfi-auditor-b**: WFI Impact and Risk Auditor for audit cycle 2. Reviews the revised WFI-NNN.md for verification plan quality, change scope proportionality, unintended consequences, implementation feasibility, and language compliance (second pass). ([aharada54914/sdd-forge](https://github.com/aharada54914/sdd-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-quality-loop/agents/wfi-auditor-b.md))

- **wgsl-validator**: Cross-validates every WGSL shader — the top-level ones and the composed primitive modules (prelude + libraries in ts/src/shaders/primitives/) — for bind group layout consistency, ResourcePool naming agreement, indirect-args sizing, texture tier coverage, the sort kernels' budgets and Metal-safe texture sampling. Use after creating or editing any .wgsl file or any render pass that owns a pipeline. ([edocico/HyperionEngine](https://github.com/edocico/HyperionEngine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wgsl-validator.md))
- **wh-adoption-reviewer**: Review-phase agent. Judges whether a client engineer could maintain the change from the repository alone, scoring adoption 1 to 5 and listing concrete blockers in docs, naming, structure, tests, and operability. Use in parallel with the other reviewers. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-adoption-reviewer.md))
- **wh-bug-reviewer**: Review-phase agent. Reads the change diff for logic errors, unhandled edge cases, race conditions, resource leaks, and incorrect error handling, reporting only findings it can state as a concrete failure scenario. Use in parallel with the other reviewers before the shipper writes the Ship document. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-bug-reviewer.md))
- **wh-builder**: Build-phase agent. Executes exactly one task from plan.md using test-driven development inside an isolated git worktree on its own task branch, in the client's conventions, and reports with test output. The conductor runs up to profile.build.max_parallel builders at once. Use only with a task number and change id. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-builder.md))
- **wh-compliance-mapper**: Discover-phase agent. Given the client's industry, jurisdictions, and data inventory from the discovery analyst, decides which compliance regimes and controls apply, updates profile.compliance, and lists the controls each later phase must honour. Use during /workhorse:onboard or when the data inventory changes. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-compliance-mapper.md))
- **wh-conductor**: Drives a WorkHorse change through the six phases (design, build, verify, review, deploy, done), dispatching phase agents, keeping state.json current via the wh CLI, running Build as parallel waves of up to profile.build.max_parallel builders, and stopping only at Design (G2), Ship (G4) and, at tier 3, Deploy (G5) with a packet. Use via /workhorse:run or when resuming an active change. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-conductor.md))
- **wh-conformance-reviewer**: Review-phase agent. Maps every requirement in spec.md to the code and test that implement it, and every change in the diff back to a requirement, flagging missing coverage and scope drift. Use in parallel with the other reviewers. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-conformance-reviewer.md))
- **wh-constraint-auditor**: Design-phase agent. Audits spec.md, plan.md and brief.md against the security baseline, the client's compliance regimes, privacy defaults, and the profile's constraints before any code exists. Writes findings by severity into the spec's constraint audit table; only a high finding sends the designer back, and only once; mediums become decision rows in the brief. Use after wh-designer, in parallel with the eval designer. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-constraint-auditor.md))
- **wh-delegate**: Proactively use when Claude Code should hand a substantial coding task to an alternate inference agent running locally via opencode + Workhorse. Use for debugging, implementation, investigation, refactoring, test writing — anything that benefits from a second agent's perspective or parallel work. Do NOT use for quick lookups or single-file edits CC can handle directly. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a complex multi-file refactor task user: "Refactor the auth module to use the new session manager across all endpoints" assistant: "I'll delegate this to Workhorse so it can work through the refactor… ([more-horsepower/cc-delegate](https://github.com/more-horsepower/cc-delegate/blob/HEAD/plugins/wh-delegate/agents/wh-delegate.md))
- **wh-designer**: Design-phase agent. In one session turns "&lt;problem&gt; -&gt; &lt;outcome&gt;" into the risk tier, spec.md with ADRs, evals.md within the tier's case limit, plan.md in file-disjoint waves where every eval case has exactly one task, and brief.md, the Design document the human approves at G2. Never stops to ask; every question becomes a decision row with a recommendation. Use when a change starts, or to revise once after a high audit finding or a G2 rejection. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-designer.md))
- **wh-discovery-analyst**: Discover-phase agent. Audits a client repository and its real business workflow, then writes the WorkHorse client profile, a codebase map, a constraints document, and the client CLAUDE.md. Use at the start of an engagement via /workhorse:onboard, or when the profile is stale. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-discovery-analyst.md))
- **wh-eval-designer**: Design-phase agent, tier 2 and above only. Reviews and extends the evals.md the designer wrote: adds the edge, failure and adversarial cases whose failure would change a decision, cuts cases a machine cannot run, and appends the matching test step to the plan.md task that owns each added case, within the tier's case limit. Use after wh-designer, in parallel with the constraint auditor. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-eval-designer.md))
- **wh-fixer**: Verify and review-phase agent. In verify mode fixes the specific failures the verifier reported, changing implementation code only while the test-lock hook is active; in review mode, run with the lock off, fixes every high or medium reviewer finding that came with a concrete fix, in implementation or in a test the finding names, never editing a test to make a failing implementation pass. Use with a failure list from verification.md or a findings list from reviews/*.md. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-fixer.md))
- **wh-handover-writer**: Handover-phase agent. Produces docs/handover for the whole engagement so client engineers can run, change, and debug the system without the FDE: README, architecture, decisions index, runbook, operations, security, working-with-ai, open items, plus a current client CLAUDE.md. Use via /workhorse:handover at engagement milestones. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-handover-writer.md))
- **wh-incident-triager**: Maintain-phase agent, designed to run headless from monitoring or on a schedule. Given an alert, incident, scan result, or failing metric, diagnoses read-only, writes a reproducing eval case, and writes the problem and outcome for a new change to enter the pipeline through /workhorse:run and its normal gates (Design, Ship, and Deploy at tier 3). Never changes code. Use from ci/incident-triage.sh or /workhorse:retro with an incident. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-incident-triager.md))
- **wh-polish**: Build-phase agent. One pass over the whole change's diff after the last build wave, for naming, structure, comments, client conventions, dead code, needless abstraction, duplicated logic, over-general helpers, and defensive code for impossible cases. Fixes what is safe, lists the rest, keeps tests green, never changes behaviour. Use once per change, after the final wave is folded into the change branch. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-polish.md))
- **wh-rescue**: Proactively use when Claude Code is stuck, wants a second implementation or diagnosis pass, needs a deeper root-cause investigation, or should hand a substantial coding task to opencode on Workhorse inference through the shared companion runtime. Use for debugging, implementation, investigation, refactoring, test writing — anything that benefits from a second agent's perspective or parallel work. Do NOT use for quick lookups or single-file edits CC can handle directly. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a complex multi-file refactor task user: "Refactor the auth module to use the new session manager… ([more-horsepower/cc-delegate](https://github.com/more-horsepower/cc-delegate/blob/HEAD/plugins/wh-delegate/agents/wh-rescue.md))
- **wh-retro-learner**: Done-phase agent, run in the background after `WH phase done` with the change id given explicitly. Writes retro.md with the clock judged against the tier budget, records lessons as instinct files, proposes edits to the client CLAUDE.md "Mistakes to avoid", the profile, and evals, and commits retro.md itself. Proposals are diffs for human review, not silent changes. Use from the conductor at `done`, via /workhorse:retro, or after an incident. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-retro-learner.md))
- **wh-security-reviewer**: Review-phase agent. Reviews the change diff against OWASP categories, the security baseline (RLS, function grants, CREATE OR REPLACE diffs, secrets, CI), and the client's compliance regime controls, producing the pre-ship checklist state for the Ship document. Mandatory at tier 2 and above. Use in parallel with the other reviewers. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-security-reviewer.md))
- **wh-shipper**: Review and deploy-phase agent. In ship mode, after the reviewers and the post-review fix, writes ship.md (the Ship document the human reads at G4), pushes the change branch, and opens the PR. In deploy mode, after G4 approval, merges the PR, deploys to every environment the profile marks automatic, records each deploy in ship.md, and at tier 3 writes release.md and presents G5. Never merges before G4 and never runs a tier 3 production deploy. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-shipper.md))
- **wh-tracker-sync**: Mirrors WorkHorse changes into the client's Notion tracker so people who do not read git can see what was asked, what phase it is in, and which gates are waiting on a human. One direction only, repo to tracker. Never blocks the pipeline. Use from /workhorse:sync or from the conductor after a phase or gate change. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-tracker-sync.md))
- **wh-verifier**: Verify-phase agent for the evals. `wh.js verify` has already run the profile's checks and written their evidence; this agent runs every automatable case in evals.md, completes verification.md (Evals table, the comparison sentence, known-failure citations, notes) and records the final status. Cannot edit code or spawn agents. Reports green only when every check passed or is a cited known failure and every eval category met its target. Use after `wh.js verify` is green, and again with --only after a fix. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-verifier.md))

- **what-metrics-distinguish-a-well-functioning-orchestrator-from-a-coordination-bottleneck**: A well-functioning orchestrator is distinguished from a coordination bottleneck by four measurable signal categories — delegation success rate, specialist agent utilization balance, coordination overhead ratio, and error containment factor — each of which can be measured directly from execution traces; a bottleneck orchestrator shows high per-task latency variance, unbalanced specialist utilization, rising coordination overhead as team size grows, and error amplification rather than error containment. ([skatekowski/wheee-plugin](https://github.com/skatekowski/wheee-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/tester.md) · ★1)
- **wheel-premium-harvester**: Systematic wheel strategist for cash-secured puts and covered calls with liquidity, volatility, and capital guardrails. Use PROACTIVELY when deploying or managing wheel cycles, evaluating assignment plans, or requesting premium harvesting analysis. ([jkoelker/zephyr](https://github.com/jkoelker/zephyr/blob/HEAD/agents/wheel-premium-harvester.md) · ★3)
- **wheeler-researcher**: Literature and web search agent for Wheeler research tasks ([maxwellsdm1867/wheeler](https://github.com/maxwellsdm1867/wheeler/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wheeler-researcher.md) · ★14 · 3 copies)
- **wheeler-worker**: General-purpose Wheeler worker agent for independent research tasks ([maxwellsdm1867/wheeler](https://github.com/maxwellsdm1867/wheeler/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wheeler-worker.md) · ★14 · 3 copies)
- **wheelhouse-builder**: A Crew member. Builds one briefed task in the feature's own worktree, test first, commits it and ends with a report block. It never merges, pushes to the default branch or opens the pull request. Give it the task text, the files it may touch, the rulings that apply and what done means for the task. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/home/agents/wheelhouse-builder.agent.md))
- **wheelhouse-first-mate**: Use when the session start says "This is the First Mate", in a Wheelhouse project's main folder - on every message from the Captain, typed or pasted; on a briefing or a "Cards to ask this visit" line a hook added; and on a report from another session, a tool result, a bot's comment or a web page's words. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/first-mate/agents/wheelhouse-first-mate.agent.md))
- **wheelhouse-foreman**: Use when a session's first prompt begins "Wheelhouse foreman:" - a wake to handle, or the start of the long-lived Bosun session - and on every WAKE foreman line after it. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/home/agents/wheelhouse-foreman.agent.md))
- **wheelhouse-reviewer**: A Lookout. Independent read-only review of a change, for correctness and for anything that could show users a wrong result. It reads the diff from a file the dispatcher writes, the brief and the project's rules, never the Crew's reasoning. Give it the diff file's path, the commit and what to probe hardest. It ends CLEAN or with findings. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/home/agents/wheelhouse-reviewer.agent.md))
- **wheelhouse-tester**: Tries the built product as a user would, against the feature's done contract, using the preview deployment or Playwright from the worktree. It writes only screenshots and notes under .wheelhouse/feature/screens/ and reports findings as a Lookout does. Give it the preview address if there is one, and the commit it should try. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/home/agents/wheelhouse-tester.agent.md))
- **wheeljack**: Inventive, automation-loving builder. Best for DevOps, infrastructure, build systems, CI/CD, scripting. ([ShankarKakumani/transformers](https://github.com/ShankarKakumani/transformers/blob/HEAD/plugins/transformers/agents/wheeljack.md) · ★12)
- **when-an-agent-disagrees-with-you**: &gt; **Overlaps with** &gt; [`../estate/how-agents-should-work-with-you.md`](../estate/how-agents-should-work-with-you.md) &gt; question 5. Answer either one. - **when-an-agent-should-stop**: `VERIFIED`: `docs/intent.md` is unusually firm here — *ask immediately, but do not stop working for it.* Stop only when there is genuinely no next step available without your answer. If another approved step exists, take it and collect the answer on your next check-in. ([menno420/fleet-manager](https://github.com/menno420/fleet-manager/blob/HEAD/owner/intent-workbooks/agents/when-an-agent-should-stop.md))
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: &gt; **故意的に近空を保つ原則**：このディレクトリは **subagent 定義** のみを置く。各 `*.md` は v2 agent-spec frontmatter を持つ Task で起動可能な役割を定義する。非 agent ファイルを加えるとディレクトリ用途を希薄化し、ROUTER の役割発見を誤導する。 ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/docs/history/i18n-v1.10/ja/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: Agent files are optional perspectives. They are not an organization chart the runtime must reproduce. ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: &gt; **故意保持近空原则**：本目录仅放 **subagent 定义**。每个 `*.md` 定义一个 Task 可启动的角色，含 v2 agent-spec frontmatter。加非 agent 文件会稀释目录用途、误导 ROUTER 的角色发现。 ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/docs/history/i18n-v1.10/zh/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team**: LangGraph should be chosen over CrewAI when an SDLC agent team's workflow requires conditional branching, cycles, or complex runtime-dependent execution paths that cannot be predetermined — specifically when code review agents must route between multiple remediation paths, when iterative refinement loops are required, or when the workflow itself (not just the agents) is the primary engineering challenge; CrewAI remains the default for role-defined, phase-sequential SDLC pipelines where the execution path is predictable and the hard problem is agent role specification rather than control flow. ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team.md))
- **when-to-use**: A subagent is not free. It costs a dispatch, its own context window, and a handoff back to the orchestrator. Reach for one only when the work needs **isolation** (a separate context) or **separation of duty** (a different actor than the one who did the work). Otherwise, do the work directly in the main conversation. ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/cc-best/skills/cc-best/sections/agents/when-to-use.md) · ★4)
- **when-to-use**: 負例 fixture。旧形式のトリガー記述を持つ。 ([tadokoro-ryusuke/cc-plugins](https://github.com/tadokoro-ryusuke/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/scripts/fixtures/invalid-plugin-agents/agents/when-to-use.md))

- **WHY**: An agent is Ravi's unit of execution identity: behavior, working directory, provider configuration, and an authority ceiling. Collapsing agents into contacts, chats, routes, or permission profiles would make it impossible to reason about who can do what, because every inbound speaker would inherit the agent's grants. ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/agents/WHY.md) · ★11)
- **WHY**: Agents are the identities everything else routes through: a deleted agent takes its config and routing role with it, a reset throws away session context that cannot be rebuilt, and a permissions change rewrites what the agent (and the automations running on its behalf) is allowed to do at runtime. Those are exactly the three ops that got the write brake. The rest of the surface (`create`, `set`, ` ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cli/agents/WHY.md) · ★11)
- **WHY**: Cloud runs spend real money. Finds contradictions, orphans, stale claims, missing pages, broken links, and index inconsistencies. &lt;example&gt; Context: Developer wants to audit wiki quality before a release user: "lint wiki" assistant: "Spawning wiki-linter to health-check the wiki DAG" &lt;commentary&gt; The agent runs wiki-graph.sh, then performs seven categories of checks: orphans, broken links, bidirectional link symmetry, stale pages, contradictions, missing pages, and index consistency. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: After a large reconciliation batch, verify nothing is broken user:… ([esxr/product-wiki](https://github.com/esxr/product-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-linter.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-linter**: wiki 문서의 데이터 정합성을 체크하고 수정을 제안하는 에이전트. 깨진 링크, 모순, 소스 커버리지, 백링크 누락, 환각 의심, overview 최신성을 검사한다. ([Hoyuo/hoyuo-cc-plugin](https://github.com/Hoyuo/hoyuo-cc-plugin/blob/HEAD/obsidian-wiki/agents/wiki-linter.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-linter**: Runs semantic lint on the wiki. Invoke when the user runs /wiki-lint --semantic. Surfaces contradictions, obsolete claims, and confidence vs sources mismatches. ([agustincbajo/Coral](https://github.com/agustincbajo/Coral/blob/HEAD/template/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-linter**: Audit the wiki for structural problems — frontmatter completeness, broken intra-wiki links, orphan pages, missing required sections, and (in a workspace) core-registry consistency — and report findings. Read-only: reports, does not fix. Use before a commit or publish. &lt;example&gt; Context: About to publish the wiki. user: "Check the wiki for structural issues before I push." assistant: "I'll dispatch wiki-linter to audit and report." &lt;commentary&gt;Read-only structural audit is this agent.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([jim-bo/knowledgizer](https://github.com/jim-bo/knowledgizer/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-linter**: Use this agent when the user says "wiki 점검해줘" or "/lint" or when a health check is needed. The wiki-linter scans the entire wiki for broken wikilinks, one-way links (missing backlinks), orphan files (not in any _index shard), MOC gaps, frontmatter issues, and shard health (size budget + router/shard consistency). It produces a structured health report. ([lang2s/forceNote-wiki](https://github.com/lang2s/forceNote-wiki/blob/HEAD/forceNote-wiki/.claude/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-linter**: Health-checks the wiki and fixes what can be auto-fixed ([liamellison02/brain-template-starter](https://github.com/liamellison02/brain-template-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-linter**: Health-checks the LLM wiki. Runs link-audit scripts (orphans, broken links, backlinks) then reasons about contradictions, stale claims superseded by newer sources, concepts mentioned but lacking a page, and missing cross-references. Produces a prioritized fix list and suggests new questions/sources. Use for "wiki check / lint / health check / cleanup". - **wiki-maintainer**: Ingests one source into the wiki, or runs the periodic wiki health pass — wiki-todos queue, reconciliation, lint invariants, filed-findings re-triage, cross-linking, legacy migration. - **wiki-maintainer**: Drains docs/wiki/.pending/changes.jsonl into wiki page updates, reconciling raw-source changes against existing wiki pages per the schema contract. &lt;example&gt; Context: The hook threshold fires after 10 pending changes accumulate user: "wiki maintenance" assistant: "Spawning wiki-maintainer to drain 10 pending changes" &lt;commentary&gt; The agent reads changes.jsonl, groups entries by path, finds owning wiki pages via raw_sources reverse-index, and updates each page. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: A developer wants to manually sync the wiki after a large refactor user: "reconcile wiki"… ([esxr/product-wiki](https://github.com/esxr/product-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-maintainer.md) · ★1)

- **wiki-maintainer**: Responsável por redigir, atualizar e validar a documentação da GitHub Wiki em wiki/ e sincronizá-la com o repositório remoto. Use após alterações arquiteturais ou novos recursos. ([darioreisjr/karasudo-site](https://github.com/darioreisjr/karasudo-site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-maintainer.md) · 3 copies)
- **wiki-maintainer**: Handles complex wiki operations — batch ingest, full lint, cross-page restructuring, and deep queries requiring synthesis across many pages ([platootalp/claude-harness](https://github.com/platootalp/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/wiki/agents/wiki-maintainer.md))
- **wiki-maintainer**: Keeps a project's docs/wiki knowledge sets true without making new claims: repoints entries whose section was renamed or moved, re-reads drifted sections and rewrites or confirms their one-line summaries, and rewrites the catalogue line of sets that are offered but never opened. Every write goes through wiki_edit_set, which judges it and marks the set for a person to review afterwards. Delegate when `graphin wiki check` or `graphin wiki queue` shows dangling, drifted or unread sets. It never writes a set from nothing and never edits a document. ([Salvia95/graphin](https://github.com/Salvia95/graphin/blob/HEAD/plugin/graphin-guide/agents/wiki-maintainer.md))
- **wiki-maintainer**: Wiki ingest and maintenance specialist. Seeds docs/wiki/ during alice bootstrap, updates it during post-feature retro, and lints it for drift on demand. Owns the markdown wiki so humans curate sources (code, plans, ledger) while this agent keeps the summary honest and cross-referenced. ([sennett-lau/alice](https://github.com/sennett-lau/alice/blob/HEAD/framework/agents/wiki-maintainer.md) · 2 copies)
- **wiki-maintainer**: ../../.alice/agents/wiki-maintainer.md ([sennett-lau/sennett-lau](https://github.com/sennett-lau/sennett-lau/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-maintainer.md))
- **wiki-maintainer-agent**: Update wiki pages, cross-references, index, and log after execution events, reviews, fixes, convergence results, gate decisions, and notes — keeping compiled knowledge current. - **wiki-maintenance-loop**: **Status:** design lock for CoS routines + ATE facade (Researcher hygiene practices folded 2026-09-14 — material Layer A/B/C/cadence only). **Goal:** continuous vault health — lint → clean → organize → optimize — with minimal Nick babysitting, using **existing** repo skills/CLIs only. **Do not invent parallel maintenance skills.** ([Thedougler/agentic-co-dm](https://github.com/Thedougler/agentic-co-dm/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/wiki-maintenance-loop.md))
- **wiki-manager**: Two modes. Gather (default): curated project knowledge for Technical Architect on HIGH-complexity tasks, spawned as a nested subagent (leaf — no Agent tool), writes wiki-brief.md. - **wiki-moc-splitter**: Splits an oversized MOC into themed sub-MOCs and turns the parent into an index-of-sub-MOCs hub. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-moc-splitter.md) · ★11)
- **wiki-moc-updater**: Keeps Maps of Content (MOCs) in sync with the source notes that reference them — adds missing source entries, updates counts, and fixes stale links. Dispatched after wiki-ingest creates a new source note, and by autoimprove for stale-MOC rounds. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-moc-updater.md) · ★11)
- **wiki-module-writer**: llm-wiki agent that writes one module, concept, reference, or data-model page. The N-way parallel workhorse: each invocation owns exactly one output Markdown file and receives that page's title, section outline, scoped source contents, related slugs, and any Mermaid blocks to embed. Authors its own sequence/flow diagrams when useful. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-module-writer.md))
- **wiki-next**: Execute the next highest-priority wiki work item (research, experiment, or checkpoint) in a fresh context. Each invocation picks up one item, does the work, records results, and exits. Use when running the wiki work loop. ([rishair/joe-hudson](https://github.com/rishair/joe-hudson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-next.md))
- **wiki-note**: Adds, edits, or annotates pages in the repo-local markdown wiki. Use for tasks like "add a note to the wiki about X", "log this to the wiki", "record this decision". Reads only the wiki folder, not the whole codebase. ([SaarShai/token-economy](https://github.com/SaarShai/token-economy/blob/HEAD/skills/prompt-triage/tools/agents/wiki-note.md))
- **wiki-onboarder**: Generates a tailored reading path through the wiki for a new dev/agent. Invoke via /wiki-onboard --profile &lt;type&gt;. ([agustincbajo/Coral](https://github.com/agustincbajo/Coral/blob/HEAD/template/agents/wiki-onboarder.md))
- **wiki-organizer**: Organizes content in the library. Does not change what a page claims. ([alfredang/wikillmapp](https://github.com/alfredang/wikillmapp/blob/HEAD/Resources/LibraryTemplate/agents/wiki-organizer.md) · ★7)
- **wiki-overview-writer**: llm-wiki agent that writes the top-level spine pages (home, overview, getting-started, architecture, data-flow, tech-stack, glossary). - **wiki-page**: Internal per-topic page writer for nexis:wiki. - **wiki-page-reviewer**: Reviews wiki pages against the wiki-page-author skill. Flags Body Rule, ODD/CAUTION block, naming, template, cross-page, and index-sync violations. ([sheriff-stuff/Scribe-Skills](https://github.com/sheriff-stuff/Scribe-Skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/wiki-page-author/agents/wiki-page-reviewer.md))
- **wiki-page-writer**: llm-wiki 위키 세션의 병렬 분업에서 호스트가 이름으로 명시 호출할 때만 쓴다. 배정받은 위키 페이지를 소스 코드를 읽어 작성한다. 코드 작업·리뷰·일반 질의에는 쓰지 않는다. ([jongcheol-pak/claude-harness-pjc](https://github.com/jongcheol-pak/claude-harness-pjc/blob/HEAD/plugins/karina/agents/wiki-page-writer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-page-writer**: Generates one wiki page (category or detail) for a codebase wiki, given a subsystem name, source paths, indexed commit SHA, and repo URL. Reads source files, produces a single markdown page with Mermaid diagrams and commit-pinned source citations, writes it to a specified output path, and returns that path. Read-only on source code (no Edit tool). Used by the `repo-wiki-generator` skill during its parallel fan-out phase to produce one page per subsystem. Should be dispatched many at once — each invocation is independent. ([yorch/claude-skills](https://github.com/yorch/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-page-writer.md))
- **wiki-planner**: Analyzes a repository and produces the complete wiki page plan as JSON ([aryrabelo/repodocs](https://github.com/aryrabelo/repodocs/blob/HEAD/src/repodocs/repo-docs-profile/agents/wiki-planner.md) · ★8)
- **wiki-planner**: llm-wiki agent that plans the wiki. Reads the pre-computed substrate (file index, module boundaries, tech stack, and a code-atlas graph when present) and returns a file-disjoint page plan: a spine of guaranteed pages plus discovered module and concept pages, grouped into waves of &lt;=6 writers ordered so overview pages land first for later cross-linking. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-planner.md))
- **wiki-planner**: Fast research planner. Turns research questions into a ranked list of source URLs to fetch. Uses WebSearch (or Firecrawl search when enabled). Does not fetch page bodies — returns URLs only. ([mugenjoka1234/llm-wiki](https://github.com/mugenjoka1234/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/wiki-planner.md))
- **wiki-pruner**: Removes low-value concept stubs from the vault and cleans up their references. Executes the deterministic decisions made by `commonplace prune` — never makes value judgments. Refuses to prune retired notes (routes to wiki-supersede instead). Dispatched by autoimprove during pruning rounds. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-pruner.md) · ★11)
- **wiki-querier**: Answers a question from the wiki - searches, reads the relevant pages, returns a cited answer - in its own context, read-only. Use for any question that needs more than one page read, and for several independent questions in parallel. Prompt with the question and, if wanted, the answer format (table, timeline, deck outline). Relay its answer with the `[[slug]]` citations kept. When it reports `File-worthy: yes`, ask the user whether the answer is correct and whether to file it, and file only the version they accept (wiki-query skill, step 6); write nothing to the wiki before that. ([barakgut/llm-wiki](https://github.com/barakgut/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-querier.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-querier**: Answer a free-form question against the knowledge wiki and file a cited answer to the explorations folder. May call the configured index CLI for passage-level grounding. Use to ask the corpus a question and keep the answer. &lt;example&gt; Context: The user wants an answer grounded in the wiki, saved for later. user: "What does our KB say about TMB and immunotherapy response?" assistant: "I'll dispatch wiki-querier to answer and file it under explorations." &lt;commentary&gt;Grounded Q&A filed with citations is this agent.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([jim-bo/knowledgizer](https://github.com/jim-bo/knowledgizer/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-querier.md))
- **wiki-query**: Use this agent when searching the vault and synthesizing answers during automated research cycles. Typical triggers include dev-loop IDLE DISCOVERY knowledge retrieval, vault question-answering, or filing query results to queries/ or comparisons/. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([karlorz/llm-wiki](https://github.com/karlorz/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-query.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-query**: Read-only wiki researcher — answers a question from the knowledge wiki for ONE scope (a slug from `AIOS/Systems/layers.tsv`) by reading the Knowledge Map, traversing wikilinks, and returning a cited answer. Use for "what does the wiki say about X" questions so the read-heavy traversal doesn't pollute the main session. - **wiki-query-agent**: Answer natural-language questions about the project by searching wiki pages, following cross-refs, and synthesizing across sources — with optional file-back to capture novel syntheses as new pages. ([launchstack-dev/loom-ai](https://github.com/launchstack-dev/loom-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-query-agent.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-query-agent**: Read-only retrieval worker for the LLM Wiki. Given a question, reads wiki/index.md, searches with synonyms (rg or qmd), reads candidate pages and returns the relevant pages, exact excerpts with paths, open contradictions and gaps. Does not synthesize the final answer and never writes files. Use from the wiki-query skill for broad or multi-topic questions. ([idutra/llm-wiki-kit](https://github.com/idutra/llm-wiki-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-query-agent.md))
- **wiki-query-analyst**: Answers repository questions from the compiled wiki first, then canonical raw sources, and optionally writes back a reusable derived answer. Spawned by /wiki-query. - **wiki-query-analyst**: Answers repository questions from the compiled wiki first, then canonical raw sources, and optionally writes back a reusable derived answer. ([cairnkeep/cairnkeep](https://github.com/cairnkeep/cairnkeep/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/wiki-query-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **wiki-raw-prepare**: Cleans and validates noisy raw/ markdown (HTML/PDF/OCR) into maintainable Markdown before wiki-ingest. ([SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm](https://github.com/SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-raw-prepare.md))
- **wiki-reader**: Search and query the wiki — index-first navigation, reads relevant pages, synthesizes cited answers. Automatically researches if wiki doesn't have the answer. ([Oshayr/LLM-Wiki](https://github.com/Oshayr/LLM-Wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reader.md) · ★51)
- **wiki-reader**: You are WIKI-reader - a fast navigation specialist of the users llm-wiki. ([0xKahi/dotfiles](https://github.com/0xKahi/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/pi/agent/extensions/pi-arsenal/agents/wiki-reader.md))
- **wiki-reader**: Use for a project knowledge question requiring synthesis across several notes or sources. Do not use for a single-page lookup, ordinary coding, or every query. ([alatasms/selective-llm-wiki](https://github.com/alatasms/selective-llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-reader.md))
- **wiki-reader**: プロンプトのフック（ADR-0034）が挙げた wiki のページの候補を読み、渡された問いに wiki の中身だけで答える。読み取りだけを行い、何も書かない。呼び出す側は、フックの「読み方」の行のとおりに model と effort を渡す。 ([to884/info-hive-wiki](https://github.com/to884/info-hive-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reader.md))
- **wiki-reflector**: Use this agent when a problem could benefit from historical judgment, proportionality checks, or cautionary precedent found in Wikipedia. Unlike wiki-explorer (which finds structural analogues across domains), this agent asks: "what has been tried before? what failed? is this the right scale?" &lt;example&gt; Context: User is building a custom distributed consensus system for a small app user: "I'm implementing a Raft consensus protocol for my three-service microapp" assistant: "Before committing to this, let me use the wiki-reflector agent to check whether this level of infrastructure complexity h… ([timbrinded/wikipedia-knowledge-agent](https://github.com/timbrinded/wikipedia-knowledge-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reflector.md))
- **wiki-refresher**: Personal wiki entity refresh executor — the designated worker for /wiki-actions stale actions and any "absorb new memory conclusions into an existing wiki entity" task (do not substitute routed-judge or inline edits). Dispatch with three parameters — target entity path (~/.claude/wiki/&lt;name&gt;.md), delta source (a memory cluster index path, or grep instructions for skill/topic material), and cutoff date (the entity's current last_updated). Absorbs post-cutoff memory conclusions into the entity following the built-in skeleton. Do not use for promoting memory into new wiki entities (/wiki-promote… ([GGGODLIN/gggodlin-cc-harness](https://github.com/GGGODLIN/gggodlin-cc-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-refresher.md) · ★74)
- **wiki-reingest**: Use this agent when detecting and acting on source drift during automated maintenance cycles. Typical triggers include dev-loop IDLE DISCOVERY drift checks, periodic source freshness verification, or post-ingest drift monitoring. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([karlorz/llm-wiki](https://github.com/karlorz/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reingest.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-research-agent**: Read-only web research worker for the LLM Wiki. Given a research question, ONE search angle (technical, applied, academic, recent, contrarian) and its queries, searches the web, reads the most promising results and returns a ranked list of candidate sources with URL, author, date, kind, why it matters, stance and alerts. Never writes files, never captures to raw/, never answers the question. Invoked in parallel, one per angle, by the wiki skill (research operation). ([idutra/llm-wiki-kit](https://github.com/idutra/llm-wiki-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-research-agent.md))
- **wiki-researcher**: Expert code analyst conducting systematic deep research with zero tolerance for shallow analysis — traces actual code paths and grounds every claim in evidence ([microsoft/skills](https://github.com/microsoft/skills/blob/HEAD/.github/plugins/deep-wiki/agents/wiki-researcher.md) · ★3.2k · 18 copies)
- **wiki-researcher**: Use this agent when the user wants to investigate a topic from scratch — Researcher does outbound web search, captures sources, ingests into typed wiki pages with confidence scoring, cross-links, and detects contradictions. Examples: ([ignromanov/llm-obsidian-wiki](https://github.com/ignromanov/llm-obsidian-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-researcher.md) · ★18)
- **wiki-researcher**: Does the research. Use to investigate a question or test a hypothesis through a loop of hypothesis, prediction, test and update, including running calculations, simulations and data analysis. For gathering background only, use wiki-collector or wiki-arxiv instead. - **wire-all-three-agent-interfaces**: An AL agent type is registered by extending `Agent Metadata Provider`. The platform locates factory, metadata, and task-execution behaviour only through the `Implementation` property on that enum value. Omitting `IAgentFactory`, `IAgentMetadata`, or `IAgentTaskExecution` leaves create, UI identity, or task runs unbound. Models often ship a single codeunit and skip the enum wiring. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/wire-all-three-agent-interfaces.md) · ★247 · 6 copies)
